parent
f0a9fe1244e730f0659a111362a62a16bbbc8104 [ diff ] 
views: Defer MenuRunnerImpl destruction when released in OnMenuClosed

MenuRunnerImpl::OnMenuClosed cleared `controller_` before invoking
ExecuteCommand and OnMenuClosed on the MenuDelegate. When a delegate
reset its MenuRunner inside those callbacks while MenuController frames
were active on the stack, MenuRunnerImpl::Release saw a null
`controller_` and deleted `this` synchronously instead of calling
DeferMenuRunnerDestruction, causing a UAF on MenuItemView.

Keep the `controller_` WeakPtr across OnMenuClosed and invalidate
`weak_factory_` when deferring destruction in Release.

Bug: 574516887
TAG=agy
CONV=cced0104-bc4a-465a-9306-2ed8a06093b5

parent
d3d5ad8bdc6fa96bbc785eb7504eebcff2b14aad [ diff ] 
[updater][win] Tolerate caption button creation failures

The installer UI thread CHECK-crashes in CaptionButton::Create() when
::SetWindowSubclass() returns FALSE for the caption BUTTON that was
created a few instructions earlier on the same thread. The crash
signature updater::ui::CaptionButton::Create() has 2,300+ reports at a
flat ~20/day since 150.0.7863.0, the first installer build that
subclasses the caption buttons through comctl32 (crrev.com/c/7839599).

The failure is environmental: 93 of the last 100 reports have 360
Total Security's safemon.dll injected into the process (0 of 100 in
other installer crashes), and the rest have other endpoint security
hooks. A few older reports fail at the BUTTON or tool tip
::CreateWindowExW() instead, which is the same class of problem.

Nothing requires these calls to succeed. The title bar already checks
IsWindow() before touching a caption button and lays out around an
absent one, so the dialog stays usable through the system menu and
keyboard shortcuts.

CaptionButton::Create() now logs and returns nullptr when it cannot
create or subclass the BUTTON, destroying the BUTTON in the latter
case, and continues without a tool tip when it cannot create the tool
tip window.

Create() destroys rather than keeps a BUTTON it could not subclass:
without the subclass, nothing clears SubclassedWindow::hwnd_ on
WM_NCDESTROY, and Windows recycles HWND values, so the stale handle
could later alias an unrelated window. IsWindow() cannot detect that,
and ~SubclassedWindow() would remove that window's subclass. To make
the invariant hold for every SubclassedWindow, SubclassWindow() now
sets hwnd_ only after ::SetWindowSubclass() succeeds.

The CHECKs also served as the crash signal that attributed this to
safemon.dll. The failures are now only visible in the installer log,
which is acceptable because nothing actionable remains on the updater
side.

::SetWindowSubclass() cannot be made to fail for a window that
Create() has just created on the calling thread, so the new test
covers the subclass failure one level down: it drives
SubclassWindow() against a window owned by another thread, which
::SetWindowSubclass() refuses.

R=ganesh@chromium.org

Test: CaptionButtonCreateTest.ReportsFailureWithoutAParent
Test: SubclassedWindowTest.FailedSubclassLeavesHwndNull

6f6505e202e48b839578a4e085be659d1fb0bf84 [ diff ] 
Make nested FrameSwapScope inert

It is possible to have multiple FrameSwapScope instances on the stack.
In particular, while navigating an iframe, FrameLoader::CommitNavigation
fires event listeners which may remove and re-insert the <iframe> to
trigger RenderFrameImpl::SynchronouslyCommitAboutBlankForBug778318,
which runs a nested CommitNavigation for the same frame.

Two web tests exercise this codepath and would fail if FrameSwapScope
tried to enforce non-reentrancy (attempted in crrev.com/c/8577800 ):

  editing/execCommand/italic-crash-by-iframe-load.html
  http/tests/misc/recreate-location-after-detach.html

Have FrameSwapScope check for a swap in progress and do nothing in that
case, so that the inner scope does not fire change notifications and
reset is_swapping_frames_ while the outer scope is alive.

Also fix a mistake in a comment in FrameLoader::CommitNavigation, which
seems to be saying the opposite of what it intended.

Bug: 41348227

parent
bdf96a68146fddf509926299fb6949c2cdbc0235 [ diff ] 
[build] Disable siso cloud trace on linux-build-service

On the 384-vCPU linux-build-service bot, siso exhausts the Cloud Trace
write quota ("Span quota exhausted", 4800 writes/min) within minutes of
starting a build. The trace exporter's bundler then backs up, and since
clog shares the bundler mutex path, ordinary log calls on the build
path block behind it: the block profile of a failed build shows ~38% of
all mutex wait in the exporter bundler and ~85 goroutine-hours blocked
in clog -> cloud logging Logger.Log. The visible symptom is siso
aborting with "scandeps: too slow" (a single fsview find taking
minutes), which fails ~5 of 6 builds. See b/575633282.

The trace data is unusable at this scale anyway, so turn it off for
this builder. The remaining contention points (traceStats mutex,
runtime lock spinning at GOMAXPROCS=384, default limits scaling
linearly with CPUs) are to be fixed in siso itself.

Bug: 575633282, 571342297

parent
ef55ae4d8ee37c064a3b96efc707b127121773f2 [ diff ] 
[Omnibox] Fix vertical cursor keys when wrapped text hides dropdown

When the UrlBar text wraps across multiple lines without preview text,
the suggestions dropdown is hidden, but vertical arrow keys (DPAD_UP /
DPAD_DOWN) were still consumed by
LocationBarMediator#handleKeyNavigationEvent before
UrlBar#moveCursorVerticallyInWrappedText could run:

1. On desktop (SUGGESTIONS_POPOVER), OmniboxSuggestionsContainer stays
visible when suggestions are suppressed while only
OmniboxSuggestionsDropdown is hidden (View.GONE), and
AutocompleteMediator#getSuggestionCount still reflects the underlying
result. Require mDropdown.isShown() in
AutocompleteCoordinator#isServingSuggestions so the autocomplete list
is not treated as selectable while hidden.
2. Return false from LocationBarMediator#handleKeyNavigationEvent when
selecting the autocomplete list fails on DPAD_UP/DPAD_DOWN instead of
falling through and returning true, and avoid waking STANDBY state on
vertical arrow keys while text is wrapping.

Fixed: 559810255

694341b0ab923d98e72df5fe59a2bcaefb7a0dfb
parent
d2fdf8a96afe9b76275c6dffa8d68ad4bae7e4a7 [ diff ] 
Enable line-end selection commands in readonly text controls

MoveToLeftEndOfLineAndModifySelection and
MoveToRightEndOfLineAndModifySelection were the only *AndModifySelection
commands gated by EnabledInEditableText, which is otherwise used by
commands that modify text. All other selection-extending commands use
EnabledVisibleSelection, which recognizes readonly text controls since
"Show caret and allow keyboard selection in readonly inputs". As a
result, Cmd+Shift+Left/Right, which macOS sends as these commands, did
nothing in <input readonly> while Shift+arrow worked.

Use EnabledVisibleSelection for both, like their siblings.

f6e2406196940c115f54b66e38d2f259e83ab889
parent
6fce02648eb9f596f214e1fe0e2f2ced4218e266 [ diff ] 
[WebUSB] Avoid redundant Windows USB completion waits

ObjectWatcher consumes an auto-reset completion signal before
WinUsb_GetOverlappedResult waits on it again. Our tests exposed
approximately 100 ms delays on ARM64, while x64 behavior masked the same
bug. That masking is not guaranteed by the WinUSB API.

Use a fresh manual-reset event and pass bWait=false after completion.
Cancellation and request lifetime are unchanged.

ARM64 traces confirm the completion delays are removed. Matched x64
transfers verified all files with no new completion stalls observed.

parent
fdd0474f102faa2cd6add222f63542bfea9383d6 [ diff ] 
[Cast Streaming] Prepare to experiment with higher max video bitrates

The 5 Mbps default ceiling for mirroring is too low for text-heavy
content on modern networks. Users report soft, hard-to-read text when
casting documents, and their experiments show that 8-10 Mbps produces
sharp text on the same network and receiver.

This CL prepares a Finch study comparing maximum bitrates of 5, 10, and
25 Mbps through the max_bitrate_mbps parameter of
kCastStreamingMaxVideoBitrate. The default remains 5 Mbps until the
study concludes. This CL:
- Starts video at the maximum bitrate, capped at 10 Mbps, instead of a
  fixed 5 Mbps, so that the quality budget is available immediately.
- Starts the usable bandwidth at the maximum bitrate, capped at 10 Mbps
  (kDefaultBitrate), so that a 5 Mbps group behaves exactly as before.
- Exposes MirrorSettings::GetMaxVideoBitrate() for the above.
- Adds the study to the field trial testing config, with the 10 Mbps
  launch candidate first.
- Makes the bandwidth tests independent of the configured maximum,
  including DoesNotCollapseBandwidthFromStaleFrameDropsBeforeStaticScreen
  from crrev.com/c/8571381.

With the default of 5 Mbps, behavior is unchanged unless the maximum is
raised through chrome://flags or Finch. When the feature is disabled,
the legacy 5 Mbps ceiling still applies.

Related changes for poor text quality and low bitrate in Cast mirroring:
  - Chromium: Prevent usable bandwidth collapse from stale frame drops
    (landed).
    https://crrev.com/c/8571381
  - Chromium: Fix false in-flight frame drops and rejected frame state.
    https://crrev.com/c/8573110
  - Chromium: Tune the encoder for screen content.
    https://crrev.com/c/8578554
  - Chromium: Prepare to experiment with higher max video bitrates
    (this CL).
    https://crrev.com/c/8576027
  - Open Screen: Make BandwidthEstimator byte based.
    https://crrev.com/c/8583477
  - Chromium: Only reduce mirroring bandwidth on real congestion
    signals.
    https://crrev.com/c/8578555

  with max_bitrate_mbps set to 5, 10, 25, and 50, and with the feature
  disabled.

Bug: 572902679, 555393284
Test: mirroring_unittests --gtest_filter=OpenscreenSessionHost*:MirrorSettings*

a84713b4a20ec627f52ccc615bf4f9359315d9fb [ diff ] 
[Autofill] Pass billing customer number from instrument manager result

After eWallet New Account Linking, PaymentLinkManager builds
InitiatePayment with the billing customer number from synced
PaymentsCustomerData. For users who had no instruments before linking
nothing is synced yet, so the request carries external_customer_id=0 and
the Payments backend rejects it.

Extend AccountLinkingResult (Java, C++ and the JNI bridge) with
billing_customer_number, populated from the InstrumentManager result,
and prefer it over the synced value when present. Behaviour is unchanged
when the server does not provide the field.

[actor] Always cache sensitive site confirmations by site

Previously, OriginGatingCache cached user confirmations according to the
CacheScope of the cache (per-origin when
kGlicNavigationGatingUseSiteNotOrigin was disabled, or per-site when
enabled). This caused repetitive user confirmation dialogs on sensitive
sites with multiple subdomains (e.g. venmo.com and auth.venmo.com).

Sensitive site confirmation is a user convenience guardrail rather than
a strict origin boundary. Once a user confirms an actor acting on a
sensitive site, they expect all subdomains on that site to be confirmed
as well.

This CL updates OriginGatingCache to always cache user confirmations by
SchemefulSite regardless of CacheScope, while preserving origin-scoped
gating for unconfirmed novel origin navigations when configured.

Reland "Checked Span: Enable tightened checking"

This is a reland of commit a27e1a2c28881022a9eb7a7d2f0d1e7f90ecafc4.
The CL (flipping a buildflag) is basically unchanged, but it now
excludes iOS, in hopes that we can get soak time on other platforms.

https://skia-review.googlesource.com/c/skia/+/1242277,
so Skia should now directly allocate a good size (from PartitionAlloc)
and not call `malloc_usable_size()` to use more than it requested. See
the linked bugs for more details.

> Checked Span: Enable tightened checking

[ReadAloud] Dispatch word boundaries from the buffered audio clock
📋 Metadata
commit
a6c758da67ad14e94a3cf2d9f0df19611d993ed0
[ log ]
author

tree
7d0e3e44249db7163039c112af39334a77898b08
parent
50e304ea48d30e9f8f493b1b64f19ee4b05f1798 [ diff ] 
[ReadAloud] Dispatch word boundaries from the buffered audio clock

WordBoundaryQueue extrapolated linearly from one anchor (media time M
audible at wall time W): due = W + (start - M) / rate. That ignores
what media::AudioClock knows about the audio buffered for output:

- After a start or flush, the clock queues the output delay as silence
  ahead of the audio. A word at media time 0 was dispatched up to one
  output delay early, on every start, flush and seek.
- After a rate change, audio written at the old rate is still queued.
  A speed-up made dispatch early, a slow-down late, until it drained.

The queue now computes each word's due time from the clock:

- start < front_timestamp():  already playing, due now.
- start <= back_timestamp():  W + TimeUntilPlayback(start), which
  counts the silence and the rate of each buffered block.
- start >  back_timestamp():  W + TimeUntilPlayback(back) +
  (start - back) / rate, for audio not written yet.

PopDueWord() and TimeUntilNextWordDue() take the clock, and the anchor
is now just the wall time the clock was last updated at
(SetAnchorTime()); the staleness guard is unchanged. PopDueWord()
returns the WordTiming, and the renderer reports its start time as
the boundary's audio_timestamp. A rate of zero or less now only stops
the wait for words in audio not written yet; buffered audio still
plays out at its own rate.

SetPlaybackRate() now replaces a sleeping pump with one that
recomputes at the new rate, resolving the TODO left for this CL.
Without it, a word written after a speed-up could be dispatched up to
kMaxExtrapolation late.

This fixes the known limitation of the previous CLs, so word
highlighting can be enabled on top of it.

Revert "[a11y] Avoid allocating DOM node IDs during accessibility removal"

This reverts commit 4bee01094f3bceb8d0132ce68f1dae2371069620.

Reason for revert: WebContentsAccessibilityEventsTest.test* keeps failing. Reverted and verified this commit is the culprit for test error https://ci.chromium.org/ui/inv/u-kaiyilin-2026-10-20-02-58-00-7deb177fbd05f73d

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-16-x64-dbg-tests/9494/overview

Original change's description:
> [a11y] Avoid allocating DOM node IDs during accessibility removal
>
> This change reduces Blink accessibility CPU time by 5.57% and overall
> renderer main thread (`CrRendererMain`) CPU time by 1.27% in Speedometer
> 3. Specifically, it reduces CPU time in `AXObjectCacheImpl::Remove` by
> 69.31%.
>
> Previously, removing a DOM node or updating its text allocated a new DOM
> node identifier during cache lookups. Untracked DOM nodes never appear
> in identifier-keyed accessibility maps. Allocating identifiers for
> untracked nodes wasted memory and CPU time. Now, Blink queries only the
> existing DOM node identifier during removal, text changes, and scroll
> updates. If a node has no identifier, Blink skips the map lookups
> immediately. This change does not alter accessibility behavior.
>
> Change-Id: Iab9114bd199a7f54d98fcab38da6bcf924e9ce0e

> Commit-Queue: Lucas Radaelli <lucasradaelli@google.com>
> Reviewed-by: David Grogan <dgrogan@chromium.org>
> Cr-Commit-Position: refs/heads/main@{#1722422}

[net] Enable QuicUseReadMultiple by default

Enable features::kQuicUseReadMultiple by default on Linux, ChromeOS, and
Android non-Cronet builds (where UDPSocketPosix::ReadMultiple()
implements batched recvmmsg() reads) while keeping it disabled on
Cronet builds (BUILDFLAG(CRONET_BUILD), as Cronet is still running its
own experiment and will launch it separately) and leaving
features::kEnableUdpGro disabled by default.

Finch Stable evaluation of EnableQuicReadMultipleAndUdpGRO shows that
batched UDP socket reads via recvmmsg (EnabledQuicUseReadMultipleOnly)
batch 3.0 to 5.2 packets per call on average and reduce QUIC RTO
timeouts (QUIC_TOO_MANY_RTOS), whereas enabling UDP_GRO with single-skb
recvmsg underperforms recvmmsg in packet batching.

Also update
QuicSessionPoolTest.MessageTooBigReadErrorDoesNotCloseConnection to
explicitly test both the single-read path (where ERR_MSG_TOO_BIG is
ignored) and the ReadMultiple path (where ERR_MSG_TOO_BIG closes the
connection to avoid dropping earlier packets in a batch); the legacy
single-read tests will be removed later once Cronet also launches
QuicUseReadMultiple. Finally, remove the EnableQuicReadMultipleAndUdpGRO
entry from fieldtrial_testing_config.json.

Bug: 515333601
Test: net_unittests

parent
048ca27079c2b1b0000fa2952fa03381965af943 [ diff ] 
[Runtime Mutability] Associate Default groups with study features

<me>

For "normal"/startup field trials, groups that don't specify any
features (e.g. Default groups) go through a special codepath where all
features of the other groups are collected and associated with the
group. See `AssociateDefaultFeatures()` [*]. This is for activation
purposes (i.e., even in a Default group, you'd only start reporting the
study/group once you query a feature).

This CL:

1. Implements similar logic for runtime mutability.

2. Removes an optimization in `AssociateDefaultFeatures()` where it
skips the aforementioned "feature association for featureless groups"
logic for ACTIVATE_ON_STARTUP (a.k.a. starts_active) studies. This
optimization exists because the only reason we do the association is for
activation purposes, but for ACTIVATE_ON_STARTUP studies, the trial is
activated immediately so there's no need to do that. However, with
runtime mutability, this doesn't work anymore. Runtime mutability highly
depends on feature associations to determine which trial gets
overridden. For example, when applying a runtime mutable study, the
trial that gets overridden is the trial that specifies the exact same
set of features. So in the above scenario, if the trial didn't get
associated with the features due to the optimizations, then it would
result in the original trial not being overridden (or even a name
collision error if the study name didn't change).

[*]
https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:components/variations/variations_seed_processor.cc;l=207;drc=93ff2586b4c5a46db432ee397cb9026dced5e1de

3. To achieve the two points above, `OVERRIDE_USE_DEFAULT` is now a
valid runtime override.

</me>

Keep ephemeral button visible when SIC open

Updates the ephemeral contextual tasks toolbar button behavior so that
it remains visible when the contextual tasks side panel is open (unless
permanently pinned), and applies an anchor highlight while the panel is
active.

- Adds anchor highlight management to ContextualTasksButton to reflect open side panel state.
- Updates ContextualTasksEphemeralButtonController to mark active task tabs as eligible when the panel is opened or tasks are associated, keeping the button visible when the panel is opened and unpinned.
- Closes the side panel when removing the button if the panel is currently open for the task.
- Updates interactive UI tests to reflect the button persisting and displaying a highlight while the side panel is open.

[ContextualTasks] Record tab picker flyout latency and Unbounded result

Add four UMA histograms for the tab picker in the Contextual Tasks
component extension:
 - ContextualTasks.TabPicker.IframeRenderLatencyMs: records time from
   iframe navigation start (performance.timeOrigin) until the
   tab-picker-app element completes its initial DOM render
   (firstUpdated).
 - ContextualTasks.TabPicker.SuggestionsLoadLatencyMs: records time from
   iframe navigation start until recent tab suggestions are fetched from
   the browser and rendered into the flyout menu.
 - ContextualTasks.TabPicker.MenuOpenLatencyMs: records time from
   hover/click triggering open until the menu is positioned and
   rendered.
 - ContextualTasks.TabPicker.UnboundedPresentationResult: differentiates
   successful presentation via the Unbounded Element API from fallbacks
   (API unsupported) and runtime presentation failures.

Also updates CrActionMenuElement to fire 'unbounded-shown' and
'unbounded-show-error' events reflecting completion of unbounded
presentation.

4393cc988e626aa74af0c372427190d561d29e3e [ diff ] 
Camera capture: Add activation free enumeration path for Windows

This change adds the Windows-only
`kWinDeviceEnumerationWithoutActivation` feature and the
`VideoCaptureDeviceEnumerationMode` enum. The feature is disabled by
default. `kDevicesWithCapabilities` preserves the existing full
enumeration behavior. When the feature is enabled on Windows,
`kDevicesOnly` returns the device list without supported formats or
pan, tilt, and zoom support.

In that mode, the Windows factory enumerates Media Foundation and
DirectShow devices without creating a Media Foundation source or
binding a DirectShow capture filter. For devices reported by both APIs,
the Media Foundation ID and device position in the list remain the same
when moving from `kDevicesOnly` to `kDevicesWithCapabilities`. If only
DirectShow reports a device's formats, the factory maps its retained
Media Foundation ID to the DirectShow ID needed to open it. This avoids
a false device-list change when capabilities are added. Non-Windows
platforms and Windows with the feature disabled continue to enumerate
fully.

This CL passes the mode through `VideoCaptureSystem` and
`VideoCaptureDeviceFactory` and implements the Windows devices-only
path. `VideoCaptureSystemImpl` now tracks each queued request's mode.
A devices-with-capabilities result can satisfy a waiting devices-only
request. A devices-only result cannot satisfy a waiting
devices-with-capabilities request. That request requires a separate
enumeration before its callback runs.
Tests cover request ordering, activation-free enumeration, and stable
device IDs and order across modes.

This CL updates code under `//media/capture`. Existing callers of
`VideoCaptureSystem` pass `kDevicesWithCapabilities` to preserve full
enumeration behavior. Follow-up CLs carry the mode from the browser
process into the video capture service.

E2E prototype: crrev.com/c/8163229

3bb14983cab81778fe4310b93a9b6bf6a747eb4b [ diff ] 
Android: Fold ProductConfig into BuildConfig

ProductConfig.java existed so Chrome and WebView could share a single
APK/binary (Monochrome) with per-package configuration. Since Monochrome
is gone and Trichrome gives Chrome and WebView separate APKs/bundles,
each binary has its own org.chromium.build.BuildConfig.

- Move LOCALES from ProductConfig to BuildConfig.
- Initialize ResourceBundle.sAvailableLocales directly from
  BuildConfig.LOCALES instead of requiring embedders to call
  ResourceBundle.setAvailablePakLocales() or setNoAvailableLocalePaks().
- Delete AwLocaleConfig.java and ProductConfig.template.
- Drop ProductConfig.USE_CHROMIUM_LINKER, using
  NativeLibraries.sUseLinker in TrichromeZygotePreload and removing
  redundant setLinkerImplementation() calls in SplitCompatApplication
  and CastBrowserApplication.

tree
5f4616cbbccb34a29fed25b9bfcfde679f7080bf
parent
f2b686dc9ef3e300ae71c0ea653295e9459b8832 [ diff ] 
[infra] Disable expensive DCHECKs on CQ trybuilders

Set `enable_expensive_dchecks = false` in the `release_try_builder` GN
args config in `//infra/config/gn_args/gn_args.star` so that release CQ
trybuilders no longer run `DCHECK`s or test hooks gated behind
`EXPENSIVE_DCHECKS_ARE_ON()`. Standard `DCHECK`s (`dcheck_always_on =
true`) remain enabled on CQ, and `enable_expensive_dchecks` remains
enabled on local developer builds and CI builders (where `is_debug =
true` or `dcheck_always_on = true`).

Also update the remaining release trybuilders in `try/*.star` (such as
`linux_chromium_asan_rel_ng` and `linux_chromium_chromeos_asan_rel_ng`)
to use `"release_try_builder"` instead of `"try_builder"`, and replace
`"release_try_builder"` / `"try_builder"` shorthand usage in `ci/*.star`
builder configs with explicit `"release_builder"`, `"dcheck_always_on"`,
and `"minimal_symbols"` configs so that CI builders keep
`enable_expensive_dchecks = true` unchanged.

See http://shortn/_DSF2ZiYgmZ for details.

f049b4cbe1855e7e2b4c3d597608dd7649b6b18a [ diff ] 
Fix CHECK failure for page activations with ad exclusion

crrev.com/c/8545611 calls FreezeExcludeAdFromOriginKeyedProcess() in
OnResponseStarted(). Back-forward cache and prerender activations also
call OnResponseStarted(), because they always use
CachedNavigationURLLoader, which gives them a cached response. The ad
decision then calls GetOriginToCommit(), which fails a CHECK because an
activation does not have a RenderFrameHost yet.

This CL makes HasSameSiteAdAncestor() return early for main frames,
before it calls GetOriginToCommit(). Page activations are always main
frames, so they no longer reach the CHECK.

WaaP: Defer 3 background observer KeyedServices until after startup

Defer eager startup creation of the following background observer
KeyedService factories behind the kLazyKeyedServiceInstantiationAfterStartup
feature parameter of kLazyKeyedServiceInstantiation, and warm them up
after browser startup completes via AfterStartupTaskUtils::PostTask in
ProfileImpl::DoFinalInit:
- ChromePingManagerFactory
- SendTabToSelfClientServiceFactory
- AutofillPrivateEventRouterFactory

Also ensures AutofillPrivateEventRouterFactory is instantiated on demand
when AutofillPrivateExtensionFunction accesses the Autofill client or
EntityDataManager, and ensures SendTabToSelfClientService is created in
AutoOpen browser/interactive tests that stay on about:blank.

parent

[infra] Fix: Use greedy siso output strategy on Linux clobber trybots

Linux clobber trybots currently often fail at "Verify Archive Paths"
with "Missing files". This step checks that every path in the archive
config exists in the local output directory, and it fails for two
reasons:

1. linux-clobber-rel and linux-chromeos-clobber-rel use the "minimum"
   siso output_local_strategy, so most outputs of remote actions (e.g.
   remotely linked binaries) are left on RBE and not downloaded to the
   local output directory. The step reports them as missing even though
   they were built successfully, so it fails even on full builds, and
   neither builder has passed in the last 30 days.
2. On most CLs, analyze prunes the compile targets, but the step still
   checks every path in the archive config. This affects all clobber
   trybots. (b:543493375)

This CL fixes 1 by switching these two builders to "greedy", which
downloads most outputs except intermediate objects and is already used
by the Windows and Android clobber trybots. 2 can be fixed by disabling
analyze on these trybots so that they always do full builds on these
trybots, as in crrev.com/c/7898341.

Bug: b:575385575,543493375

[dcheck-to-check] Convert DCHECKs in chrome/browser/ [25/25]

As part of effort to harden Chromium's safety, turn DCHECKs
into CHECKs. The converted CHECKs are not fatal until M162.

See also:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1tzGQwGG0lJ6K7Ur8wBW6E58CvJc1JQNyiX0ryHe7A5I/edit?usp=sharing

This patch is generated by the following script:
https://crrev.com/c/7849874/18/tools/memory/dcheck/replace_dchecks_with_checks.py

PSA:
https://groups.google.com/a/chromium.org/g/chromium-dev/c/vd1Qt-egELY/m/badCjDnsDAAJ
LSC (Google internal):
http://goto.google.com/chrome-lsc-dcheck-to-check-conversions

NO_IFTTT=Mechanical DCHECK to CHECK conversion

TAG=agy
CONV=a1524c3d-aa57-4e54-bb07-257a330b4888

parent
0964041e93f1712527c606cb5fdf9e821484bbd1 [ diff ] 
[Actor] Observe target surface when WaitTool fails safety checks

This CL tries to solve an issue that when calling WaitTool on an empty
tab, although the action result is correct(kUrlBlocked), it include no
tabs.

The issue is caused by that when a ToolRequest with a null
ActorSurfaceHandle fails pre-execution checks in
ExecutionEngine::KickOffNextAction() or
ExecutionEngine::DidFinishAsyncSafetyChecks(), ToolController is never
reached and WaitTool::UpdateTaskBeforeInvoke() does not run. When no
other tabs have been added to the ActorTask yet, this leaves both
controlled_tabs_ and to_observe_tabs_ empty, causing
BuildActionsResultWithObservations() to return an ActionsResult with no
TabObservation.

Changes:
- Check GetActorSurfaceForValidation() for crashed renderers in
  ExecutionEngine::KickOffNextAction(), and call
  ObserveActorSurfaceOnce() in KickOffNextAction() and
  DidFinishAsyncSafetyChecks() when GetActorSurfaceHandle() is
  null.
- Update WaitTool::UpdateTaskBeforeInvoke() to call
  ObserveActorSurfaceOnce() directly.
- Verify in WaitTool_DontRecordActOnTask and
  WaitTool_BlockedByScheme that the observed tab is recorded in
  GetLastActedTabs() without adding it to GetTabs().

43d9eb57909e479a2d9bbcb0e637b2eff7925bfa
parent
55d9ce99c71dd61f0f9a1ed31709e6352ba1de4f [ diff ] 
[net] Use per-key loading for single-domain CookieMonster deletes

`CookieMonster::DeleteCanonicalCookieAsync` and single-domain
`DeleteAllMatchingInfoAsync` previously queued tasks via the global
`DoCookieCallback`. When invoked before the persistent SQLite cookie
store finishes loading, `DoCookieCallback` sets `seen_global_task_ =
true`, which disables per-key fast loading (`LOAD_COOKIES_FOR_KEY`) for
all subsequent cookie reads and writes across all domains and forces
them to wait for the full store `LOAD`.

Under `net::features::kCookieMonsterPerKeyDelete`:
- Introduces `CookieDeletionInfo::SingleMatchingRegistrableDomain()` to
  identify deletion requests whose matching cookies are guaranteed to
  belong to a single eTLD+1 domain key.
- Routes `DeleteCanonicalCookieAsync` and single-domain
  `DeleteAllMatchingInfoAsync` through `DoCookieCallbackForHostOrDomain`
  so single-domain deletions do not set `seen_global_task_` and
  preserve per-key loading during startup.
- Records `Cookie.DeleteCanonicalCookie.Duration` and
  `Cookie.DeleteAllMatchingInfoAsync.HasSingleMatchingDomain`
  unconditionally.

Design Doc (Google Internal)
https://docs.google.com/document/d/16kKVIsWqssG33mHRw8Ym8nOv3qjsksS4zGdG8_uLqrw/edit?usp=sharing

parent
c80b6e46b5974405ad6d63812dc3cb8a1ef08a09 [ diff ] 
[Mac] Add a workaround to refresh CADisplayLink

Drain WindowServer events for CADisplayLink.

In child processes like the GPU process that lack an NSApplication event
loop, WindowServer display reconfiguration and wake events can
accumulate in the connection Mach port without being dispatched. This
can prevent CADisplayLink from updating, leading to frozen windows after
power resume (dropped VSync callbacks) or stale refresh rates following
display configuration changes.

This CL introduces CADisplayLinkMac::PumpWindowServerEvents(), which
listens on the WindowServer connection Mach port and drains pending
events using CGSMainConnectionID(), CGSGetEventPort(), and
CGEventCreateNextEvent().

Additionally, add the Viz.DisplayLink.DrainEventQueueUs histogram to
monitor the time spent draining the WindowServer event queue.

Original change's description:
> Split key pinning implementation into separate classes
>
> The approach of this change is to move pinning related implementation
> details from TransportSecurityState into KeyPinningState and subclasses,
> but TransportSecurityState still owns the instance of the
> KeyPinningState object and consumers still go through
> TransportSecurityState for all pinning related calls.
>
> Followup CLs will make further steps in the refactor of actually moving
> enforcement to be done during cert verification.

[Glic]: Reorganize //chrome/browser/glic/android into subpackages

Split //chrome/browser/glic/android into functional subpackages while
keeping a single BUILD.gn file:
- actor_login/: Actor login permissions UI and JNI bridge
- settings/: Glic settings fragment, preferences, and navigation utils
- toolbar_button/: Toolbar button controllers, split button, task menu
- ui/: Bottom sheet, side panel, promo, opt-in, and mic dialogs

[ios] Add default promo opportunity logging helper, histograms, and prefs

This CL introduces LogDefaultBrowserFullscreenPromoOpportunity and its
associated UMA histograms, which record multi-threshold score buckets
and continuous opportunity scores for the default browser video promo.

Specifically:
- Adds kIosDefaultBrowserPromoOpportunityFirstTimeCohort local state pref to distinguish first-time eligible opportunities from regular
opportunities per experiment cohort.
- Adds IOSDefaultBrowserVideoPromoOpportunity enum and UMA histograms
  (Opportunity and OpportunityScore with .FirstTime and .Regular variants).
- Adds LogDefaultBrowserFullscreenPromoOpportunity to log opportunity
  telemetry.
- Adds //components/segmentation_platform/public dependency to
  default_browser/model:utils and unit_tests.
- Updates unit tests covering score bucketing, first-time pref guarding,
  and cohort resets.

Chain order: 2/4 (Opportunity logging helper, histograms & prefs)
Landing order: 2 Parent CL:
https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8581902 Child
CL: https://chromium-review.googlesource.com/c/chromium/src/+/8574424

8572fd56cb97813871a7e183ad75825c2547fc38 [ diff ] 
Fix Glic button text contrast under dark custom extension themes.

Under dark custom extension themes, TabStripGlicButton and
ToolbarGlicButton produce dark text on dark backgrounds.
TabStripGlicButton used kColorTabSearchButtonCRForegroundFrameActive,
which statically maps to ui::kColorSysOnSurfacePrimary and does not
adapt to theme frame colors. ToolbarGlicButton used
kColorAvatarButtonHighlightDefault for its background, but left its
text colors, GetForegroundColor(), and close button icon on default
light-mode tokens.

Update TabStripGlicButton to use kColorTabForegroundInactiveFrameActive
to match TabStripControlButton and blend dynamically against inactive
tabs. Update ToolbarGlicButton to derive text and foreground colors from
kColorAvatarButtonHighlightDefaultForeground, and update its close
button icon color dynamically.

Add browser tests covering both button variants under dark extension
themes.

Fixed: 462775019

Validate stored super encrypted preference hashes

Validate the stored super encrypted hash by decrypting it and comparing
the underlying hash. Re-encrypting the same data produces a fresh nonce,
so comparing ciphertext incorrectly rejects unchanged authenticators.

Reuse the existing encrypted-hash validator and retain the filtering of
legacy HMACs. Cover persisted hashes, legacy HMAC changes, and tampering
with legacy fallback enabled and disabled.

R=wfh@chromium.org

Tue Oct 20 01:03:23 2026
tree
ed0b5d9cb5bb8ef4693745b5aa2a1ef0297223e6
parent
6f93c00293ebf96a267e1465c3947e9f8de8ee28 [ diff ] 
Recompute compositor memory policy on screen change

blink::GetGpuMemoryPolicy() computes
cc::LayerTreeSettings::memory_policy once during
WidgetBase::InitializeCompositing() based on the initial display's
screen_info.rect and device_scale_factor. When a window later moves to a
display with a different resolution or device scale factor,
WidgetBase::UpdateSurfaceAndScreenInfo() updates the LayerTreeHost's
device_scale_factor but leaves its ManagedMemoryPolicy unchanged.

When moving from a lower-resolution display (e.g., 1440x900@2x, 546 MB
budget) to a higher-resolution external display (e.g., 3008x1692@2x,
where GetGpuMemoryPolicy() would allocate 2145 MB), the renderer retains
the initial display's tile memory budget despite tile sizes scaling with
the new viewport, causing TileManager to run out of tile memory and
checkerboard visible tiles.

Plumb LayerTreeHost::SetMemoryPolicy() through Proxy, and update the
memory policy in WidgetBase::UpdateSurfaceAndScreenInfo() using
GetOriginalScreenInfos() behind
kUpdateCompositorMemoryPolicyOnScreenChange.

a6ff45faabd4e9de0a733ab4208ff73ce56cc3a9

tree
d987751deced116382286604231a6de08c0f8403
parent
146e3d633c13bcf43f7584a0b08a888e8b75402d [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Use ScopedObservation in //extensions

Replace manual AddObserver()/RemoveObserver() pairs with
base::ScopedObservation in classes that observe a single, fixed source
for their whole lifetime. This ties each observation to the source it
was added to, makes it impossible to forget to unregister, and avoids
looking up keyed services like ExtensionRegistry again just to remove
the observer. NetworkingPrivateServiceClient's NetworkConnectionTracker
observation, which uses differently named methods, is converted too
through the existing ScopedObservationTraits specialization.

Observations that were removed in Shutdown() or ShutdownOnUIThread() are
still reset there, so no source is used after the KeyedService shutdown
phase. ExtensionHost resets at the top of its destructor to keep its
existing teardown order, and ExtensionPrefStore and
MimeHandlerViewAttachHelper reset when their source is destroyed.

Observers with conditional, re-targeted or multiple sources (e.g.
EventRouter, RendererStartupHelper, the Bluetooth and networkingPrivate
event routers, WebstoreInstaller) and static observer APIs
(DevToolsAgentHost, WorkerThread) are left unchanged.

No behavior change intended.

parent
2d8fb5ddf913eb207457c1ac5a692e6ecfa371c2 [ diff ] 
[remoting] Fix UAF in WebrtcTransport::OnLocalDescriptionSet

In WebrtcTransport::OnLocalDescriptionSet,
AddPendingCandidatesIfPossible may fail when adding a queued ICE
candidate and synchronously call Close(). Close() invalidates weak
pointers, resets peer_connection_wrapper_, and notifies
event_handler_->OnWebrtcTransportError(), which on the host deletes the
WebrtcTransport instance. Previously, OnLocalDescriptionSet subsequently
called peer_connection()->GetSenders() on the freed WebrtcTransport.

Check a WeakPtr<WebrtcTransport> after AddPendingCandidatesIfPossible()
and return early if the transport was closed or destroyed.

TAG=workflow
TAG=agy
CONV=5a46d9d0-747c-4f50-943e-380663764873

[focus-without-user-activation] Remove the policy console warning

Blocked focus calls emit a feature-specific console warning in addition
to enforcing the focus-without-user-activation permissions policy. This
runtime warning conflicts with the DevTools console policy:
https://chromium.googlesource.com/devtools/devtools-frontend/+/main/docs/policy/console-policy.md

Remove the custom warning without changing the focus decision or use
counters.

[Files]: Use constexpr sets and spans in file tasks

Reduce heap allocations and string copies across Office, virtual file
task, and App Service intent handling:

- Replace NoDestructor-wrapped std::set<std::string> Office extension
  and MIME type groups with constexpr base::fixed_flat_set
  std::string_view sets exposed as base::span.
- Replace VirtualTask's matcher vectors with virtual methods returning
  base::span<const std::string_view>.
- Update file task and apps_util (intent_util) helpers and callers to
  use base::span, std::string_view, and base::flat_set instead of
  std::set and std::string.
- Fix pre-existing extension (.xslx -> .xlsx) and MIME type (duplicate
  application/vnd.ms-excel -> application/vnd.ms-powerpoint) typos in
  FileHandlerDialogBrowserTestWithAutomatedFlow's
  PreexistingFileHandlersAreNotOverwritten test.

tree
b021dbffc87061ba646304b1e5467738bc48ef9c
parent
babc807c2a2c6aa333f2f3f8af72d605f042822e [ diff ] 
[Webui Omnibox] Skip Stage 3 Escape when URL is just unelided

Issue: When the omnibox URL is unelided without user edits
(fullUrlShown_ is true and userInputInProgress_ is false), the input
value matches fullUrl_ rather than permanentDisplayText_. Comparing
solely against permanentDisplayText_ in Stage 3 (kClearUserInput)
treated the unelided URL as dirty input, requiring an extra Escape press
to revert to the elided URL before blurring and closing the UI in Stage
4 (kBlur).

Fix: Check against fullUrl_ when fullUrlShown_ is true so Escape
proceeds directly to Stage 4 when the URL is only unelided. In Stage 4,
call revert() before closing the UI if fullUrlShown_ is true (and reset
show_full_url_ in OmniboxPopupHandler::Revert) so the underlying native
omnibox view is restored to the elided display URL if it was unelided by
a drag or double-click selection.

Fixed: b:574533999

ac7c8d2ae7db39e647dca1bd3f285cc5d7dcb49c [ diff ] 
[ChroMenu] Make the search bar a container view

Turn AppMenuSearchBarView into a container that holds the search field.
A follow-up CL adds a full-width separator below the field, which the
textfield can't draw itself because the field is inset 12dp on each
side. There is no visual change.

The textfield code moves unchanged into a private nested class,
AppMenuSearchBarView::SearchField, and the search bar exposes only what
the menu needs: its text, the leading icon, and a text changed callback.
The field's margins move from the menu item into the search bar, and
tests find the field by element ID.

430253c0dc8a1f619a1cc9897a0934e33d201ad0
parent
71a792a0a056e0f66c89fd16476f9cf8db29b47e [ diff ] 
[glic-si] Remove legacy glic session open/close metrics

GlicMetrics::OnGlicWindowClose() has no callers in multi-instance glic,
so Glic.Session.Duration, Glic.Metrics.Error and the GlicSessionEnd
action are never recorded. Remove the method, the session start time it
consumed and the associated error enum.

Also stop recording Glic.Session.Open.Attached (always false in
multi-instance) and Glic.Session.Open.InvocationSource from
OnGlicWindowStartedOpening(). They are unused, and per-instance open
sources are covered by Glic.Instance.InitialInvocationSource and
Glic.Instance.{SidePanel,Floaty}.OpenSource.

Also remove the GlicSessionBegin user action, recorded from
OnGlicWindowStartedOpening(). It was the counterpart of GlicSessionEnd,
which is removed above. See http://screen/68k7piqopk1e0 for a look at
this metric from April when only the OnGLicWindowOpen session metrics
were turned back on.

This completes the removal of the single-instance Glic.Session.*
histograms recorded by GlicMetrics. The remaining Glic.Session.*
histograms are multi-instance metrics and are being renamed to
Glic.Instance.* in a separate chain (bugs 572889926 and 572890974).

OBSOLETE_HISTOGRAMS=Single-instance glic session metrics; unused or superseded by Glic.Instance.* metrics recorded by GlicInstanceMetrics.

tree
4525c9063eb892fe52db3d1cdd9d2d5e467b438e
parent
22d3a342b033594413bfd06807a195c96817d0d8 [ diff ] 
[GTK] Seed the Material palettes with the GTK accent color

With the GTK system theme, only kColorAccent and the text selection
colors followed the theme's accent. Most accented browser UI (the
omnibox focus ring, inactive tab hover, focus borders, ...) is derived
from the generated Material palettes, which stay Chrome's baseline blue
when no Chrome user color is set. This produced a mix of orange and blue
accents with e.g. Yaru.

Use the accent as the user color instead, so the standard ref and sys
mixers generate all palettes from it:
- OsSettingsProviderGtk::AccentColor() returns the portal accent if
  available, otherwise the GTK theme's selection color.
- ThemeService::GetColorProviderKey() keeps the native theme's user
  color source with a Linux system theme rather than forcing the
  baseline palette.

2f8a6d8cb04b38bd79bcf842e52891b7b69b3db7
parent
77696923e5e219b8432e82efcd58b7434bf6cfec [ diff ] 
Add TranslatedSelectionMapper for main panel selections

Because translated text cannot be mapped directly to the original PDF
text by character offsets, selections are synchronized between Reading
Mode and the main panel one block at a time.

This adds TranslatedSelectionMapper with getReadingModeSelection, which
maps a selection in the main panel to a ReadingModeSelection that
selects the whole blocks containing it in the Reading Mode panel. The
selection's endpoints are in the blocks' parents so that it stays valid
while translation rewrites the blocks' contents.

Mapping selections in the other direction will be added in follow-up
CLs. The mapper is not used yet.

b6c9c5f69f4cffccd567ccf6e1ca084c02669540 [ diff ] 
[Linux] Share command-line activation token lookup

Normal browser startup and web-app launches need to read activation
tokens from the same command-line switches. Replace the unused
ExtractXdgActivationTokenFromCmdLine() with
GetXdgActivationTokenFromCommandLine(), which returns an optional token
without modifying the command line or global storage.

Use the reader in normal browser startup. Prefer a nonempty
--xdg-activation-token value, then --desktop-startup-id. If neither
provides a token, consume the stored global token as before.

Make TakeXdgActivationToken() move the stored token out with
std::exchange instead of copying it and clearing the original.

Behaviour is unchanged.

parent
f0aa1bf56a0cf0067b370cf4547b236872cae9cd [ diff ] 
App Mall: Fix broken Japanese text on offline screen

Wrap index.html in a standard HTML document structure with
<meta charset="utf-8"> and textdirection/language attributes.

This matches other system web app HTML templates:
https://source.chromium.org/search?q=ash%2Fwebui%2Fos_feedback_ui%2Fresources%2Findex.html

Before: https://buganizer.corp.google.com/action/issues/571801828/attachments/83866391?download=false
After: https://buganizer.corp.google.com/action/issues/571801828/attachments/83866272?download=false

Without an explicit charset declaration in headers or HTML, WebUI
pages can fall back to legacy locale-dependent encodings (e.g. CP932 /
Shift_JIS on Japanese systems). This causes UTF-8 localized strings
such as IDS_ERRORPAGES_HEADING_YOU_ARE_OFFLINE to decode incorrectly
as garbled text.

Fixed: b:571801828

tree
2e10a7065705301d708a77f2281ce07af823f66f
parent
8f54c57cea6255d4677efc523a72ec35995f2145 [ diff ] 
guest_os: Ignore empty container app icon content

When an icon file inside a Crostini container is 0 bytes,
Garcon/Cicerone can return a DesktopIcon entry with an empty icon
payload. Previously, GuestOsRegistryService::OnContainerAppIcon only
checked whether the icon list was empty, not whether icons[0].content
was empty, which caused InstallIconFromFileThread to hit
DCHECK(!content.empty()) for both PNG and SVG icons.

Check for empty icon content in OnContainerAppIcon and invoke active
callbacks with an empty string instead of attempting to transcode or
write an empty icon file to disk.

Bug: 571847721
Test: unit_tests --gtest_filter="GuestOsRegistryServiceTest.RequestIconWithEmptyContent"

55b54553c3e2ac2e5a47897657a4239b34373f08 [ diff ] 
[oobe] Advance directly to enrollment in Oobe.loginForTesting

Oobe.loginForTesting() previously called skipToLoginForTesting
unconditionally before checking enterpriseEnroll. When enterpriseEnroll
was true, this navigated to GaiaScreen first (starting async DNS/cookie
clearing in GaiaScreenHandler::LoadGaiaAsync) and immediately
transitioned to EnrollmentScreen, causing ShowGaiaScreenIfReady() to
overwrite the active enrollment partition and hit a DCHECK in
EnrollmentScreenHandler::HandleCompleteLogin().

Move skipToLoginForTesting into the !enterpriseEnroll branch and advance
directly to enterprise-enrollment when enterpriseEnroll is true.

TAG=agy
CONV=c321a829-fd9a-4938-8735-f76a6b74935c

Manage visibility from layout to simplify states for TopContainer

1) For performance and simplicity reasons it's easier to manage
visibility through layout since we can use available width and state
controller states in layout to compute visibility without needing
override states and handle extra callbacks.

2) Fixed a visual bug where the tab strip doesn't shift at the end of
collapse animation with expand on hover functionality enabled.

Revert "[TabFeatures] Convert BookmarkTabHelper to std::unique_ptr"

This reverts commit 8607c3fe0e7cf75fe081680505a77f2848d039cb.

Reason for revert: Causing build failures on macOS bots. Gemini believes that the root cause is that DestroyTabFeatures() destroys BookmarkTabHelper while TouchBarNotificationBridge::bookmark_tab_helper_observation_ is still observing it.

Failure Link: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/Mac13%20Tests/43371/overview

Original change's description:
> [TabFeatures] Convert BookmarkTabHelper to std::unique_ptr
>
> Convert BookmarkTabHelper from WebContentsUserData to a tab-scoped class
> owned by TabFeatures via std::unique_ptr and registered with
> ScopedUnownedUserData on TabInterface.
>
> extensions::BookmarkManagerPrivateDragEventRouter was the only
> implementation of BookmarkTabHelper::BookmarkDrag and previously created
> a BookmarkTabHelper on non-tab WebContents (such as the Bookmarks Side
> Panel) solely so WebDragBookmarkHandlerAura and
> WebDragBookmarkHandlerMac could look up the drag delegate via
> BookmarkTabHelper::FromWebContents. Have the drag handlers look up
> BookmarkManagerPrivateDragEventRouter directly on the WebContents and
> remove BookmarkTabHelper::BookmarkDrag, so BookmarkTabHelper is strictly
> tab-scoped.
>
> Remove BookmarkTabHelper::CreateForWebContents from
> _ALLOWED_CREATE_FOR_CALLS in chrome/browser/ui/tabs/PRESUBMIT.py.
>

[Omnibox] Pass the tab favicon factory to the ORP constructor

Replace the static tab favicon factory with a final field on
OmniboxResourceProvider, supplied through a new constructor.
LocationBarCoordinator passes the factory when creating its shared
ORP, and the static setTabFaviconFactory() is removed. Tests supply
the factory to their single ORP instance, removing global state that
leaked between tests.

[base] Add EXPENSIVE_TEST_LOGIC_IS_ON() macro

Introduce `EXPENSIVE_TEST_LOGIC_IS_ON()` in `//base/dcheck_is_on.h` and
`//base:debugging_buildflags`, backed by the `enable_expensive_dchecks`
GN argument (identical to `EXPENSIVE_DCHECKS_ARE_ON()`).

This provides a semantic conditional for gating expensive test-only
checks or hooks (such as per-test spare renderer memory pressure logic)
so that they continue running on CI and local developer builds where
`enable_expensive_dchecks = true`, without slowing down CQ trybuilders
where `enable_expensive_dchecks = false`.

See http://shortn/_DSF2ZiYgmZ for details.

tree
5ec86aed0fe901ce365f288ddda5f90aa799633e
parent
e9c5ccb0e4bc35e735fb4ffc6e73925336aa0b1e [ diff ] 
[a11y] Avoid allocating DOM node IDs during accessibility removal

This change reduces Blink accessibility CPU time by 5.57% and overall
renderer main thread (`CrRendererMain`) CPU time by 1.27% in Speedometer
3. Specifically, it reduces CPU time in `AXObjectCacheImpl::Remove` by
69.31%.

Previously, removing a DOM node or updating its text allocated a new DOM
node identifier during cache lookups. Untracked DOM nodes never appear
in identifier-keyed accessibility maps. Allocating identifiers for
untracked nodes wasted memory and CPU time. Now, Blink queries only the
existing DOM node identifier during removal, text changes, and scroll
updates. If a node has no identifier, Blink skips the map lookups
immediately. This change does not alter accessibility behavior.

tree
e61f25417765f2afd15ec12029aab01d35e7cc0b
parent
d8f2d78eb24562db8293c212b29bfca03769dcfd [ diff ] 
media: Catch IllegalArgumentException in MediaDrm.restoreKeys

MediaDrm.restoreKeys() throws IllegalArgumentException when the key set
ID is no longer valid on the device, such as when the CDM's offline
license store was reset while Chrome's storage still has the entry.
MediaDrmBridge.loadSessionWithLoadedStorage() only caught
IllegalStateException, causing an uncaught Java exception in the GPU
process.

Catch IllegalArgumentException alongside IllegalStateException and pass
it to onPersistentLicenseLoadFail() so the stale storage entry is
cleared and the load promise resolves with an empty session ID. Also
reset mSessionEventDeferrer in onPersistentLicenseLoadFail() so a failed
restoreKeys() call does not leave the deferrer set.

Verified MediaDrmBridgeTests on Pixel 8a.

49722ca1f36a6f33e5e9a5e38c14c08d6c7f4a14 [ diff ] 
[focus-without-user-activation] Report permissions policy violations

The focus-without-user-activation policy check uses a non-reporting
IsFeatureEnabled overload. Focus is blocked as intended, but
ReportingObservers and configured reporting endpoints do not receive
violation reports. Report-only violations are also lost.

Use the reporting-enabled check on the initiating frame. Suppress
reporting when focus is already within that frame's subtree, where the
policy does not restrict focus, and retain the existing user-activation
and user-gesture exemptions.

tree
e8c8f0f0f538e5b6d4bd4b8b2392360a8de80ae9
parent
9ce238535f789683fd94ee9c4e29bed196c27c80 [ diff ] 
Lower NSS's minimum RSA key size instead of disabling the check

MockOwnerKeyUtil disabled NSS's key size check for signing while it held
an imported test key. That broke OwnerSettingsServiceAshTest.
SignPolicyFailure, which uses a 256-bit key to make signing fail: NSS
now attempts the RSA operation, and its error path frees the result
using an uninitialized length (NSC_SignFinal() doesn't set outlen on
failure), which MemorySanitizer reports with the Ubuntu 26.04
instrumented libraries.

Keep the check enabled and lower NSS_RSA_MIN_KEY_SIZE to 512 bits (the
size of policy::PolicyBuilder's signing keys) instead, so too-small keys
are still rejected up front. NSS_RSA_MIN_KEY_SIZE is also available in
the sysroot's NSS headers, so the fallback definitions can be removed.

🌺 Revert "Coalesce and deduplicate startup Activity.setTaskDescription IPCs"

This reverts commit ccb402ac1d43343db0d39415b0eba6fb161a849c.
Culprit: ccb402ac1d43 - "Coalesce and deduplicate startup Activity.setTaskDescription IPCs" (refs/heads/main@{#1722231}, https://crrev.com/c/8580566)

Reason for revert: consistently fails android-internal-desktop-x64-rel.

Mechanism:
StatusBarColorControllerUnitTest#testUpdateStatusBarColor_CoalescesAndDeduplicatesSetTaskDescription[33]
fails deterministically on android-internal-desktop-x64-rel (9/25
builds) and android-desktop-x64-rel (3/25 builds) with
ClassCastException: Cannot cast org.robolectric.shadows.ShadowActivity
to StatusBarColorControllerUnitTest$ShadowTaskDescriptionActivity during
ActivityScenarioRule setup (ShadowActivity.callAttach ->
Activity.setTheme -> Activity.onApplyThemeResource ->
Activity.setTaskDescription). Using Mockito.spy(mActivity) instead of a
custom @Implements(Activity.class) shadow avoids the Robolectric
ShadowActivity cast conflict.

StatusBarColorControllerUnitTest#testUpdateStatusBarColor_CoalescesAndDeduplicatesSetTaskDescription[33]
has failed in 9/25 recent builds of android-internal-desktop-x64-rel,
every attempt in every one of them. This is not flake: it reproduces on
every run, so it blocks the CQ for every unrelated CL that touches this
suite. The regression appears at refs/heads/main@{#1722231}.

Example failing run:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chrome/builders/try/android-internal-desktop-x64-rel/32738

To run/deflake this test on CQ before relanding, add the following
footer to your CL description:
  Include-Ci-Only-Tests: chrome.android.desktop:android-desktop-17-x64-rel-emu-tests,chromium.android.desktop:android-desktop-x64-rel-15-tests|chrome_junit_tests

Reverting to green the tree first. Please re-land once the failure is
understood and covered; this revert is about restoring CQ throughput,
not about the merit of the change.

pdse: Add "More options" search view to search engine list dialog

Introduces the frontend UI and handler wiring to allow users to explore
and select additional search engines beyond the regional list.

UX behavior:
- Adds a "More options" button in the top-right header of the default
  search engine picker (gated behind the pickerWithMoreEngines feature
  flag).
- Clicking "More options" replaces the regional picker dialog in place
  with a "More search engines" dialog.
- Clicking "Cancel" or pressing Esc in the "More search engines" dialog
  closes the dialog, just like for the picker dialog.
- The "More search engines" dialog provides a search field with
  diacritic-insensitive filtering on engine names and keywords, plus an
  empty state when no engines match.
- Either dialog commits its selection via setDefaultSearchEngine.
- The guest mode checkbox state now lives in search-page which passes it
  to both dialogs and allows them to share the state this way.
- The current selection is cleared if filtering or a
  list refresh hides the selected engine.

45d58fc9059ed22f51043784299d9161cc518f58
parent
532002c37cee05d83aadb8eafc63ce31be17e9f4 [ diff ] 
[svg] Fall back to masking when SVG clip path union fails

LayoutSVGResourceClipper::AsPath() combines multiple <clipPath> children
using SkOpBuilder. If the union failed, Blink ignored the failure and
cached the first child as a valid analytic clip. Painting then used only
that child, while hit testing and clip bounds continued to account for
every child, producing inconsistent rendering and interaction geometry.

Handle union failure by leaving the analytic-path cache invalid and
returning std::nullopt. This invokes the existing mask-based fallback,
which paints every contributing child and keeps painting consistent with
hit testing and intersection geometry. Use the modern
std::optional<SkPath> -returning SkOpBuilder::resolve() API rather than
its deprecated output-parameter overload.

Add a WPT reftest covering HTML and SVG clip-path consumers. It uses a
valid path ending in a trailing moveto, which causes Skia path-union
resolution to fail, and verifies that all clip-path children still
contribute to the rendered clip. Verified that the new WPT passes in
Firefox as well.

[GEiC] Split per-instance Glic web client metrics by provider

Also record the per-instance web client histograms under
Glic.Consumer.X / Glic.Enterprise.X through GlicMetricReporter, using
the instance's Profile. The combined Glic.X recordings are unchanged.
- GlicWebClientHandler: Glic.Api.*, Glic.Host.OpenedInRegularTab,
  Glic.MaybeReplaceNavigation.Result and
  Glic.Instance.AutoOpenedPanel.FirstAction.*.
- Drag and drop: Glic.DragAndDrop.*. The reporter is created before the
  async context fetch and bound into the callback.
- GlicInstanceHelper: Glic.Tab.Instance{Bind,Pin}Count.

histograms.xml declares the converted names as Glic{GlicProvider}.X.
User actions are unchanged. Stay combined: Glic.Api.UseCounter (dynamic
name), Glic.Api.RequestHostLatency.{ApiRequestType} (recorded on every
API call), and the webview-only Glic.Contents.* histograms
(Glic.Contents.{NavigationCommitTime2,LoadCompleteTime2} and
Glic.Contents.WebUiCrash.TerminationStatus).

215d848e7a24fbfd1488c0e86344f461f14935d6 [ diff ] 
[nastaliq] Support Arabic Nastaliq font selection on Android

Map `USCRIPT_ARABIC_NASTALIQ` to "und-Aran" in `ToSkFontMgrLocale()` and
pass `script_for_font_` in `LayoutLocale::LocaleForSkFontMgr()` so
`SkFontMgr_Android` matches the `lang="und-Aran"` fallback entry in
Android's `fonts.xml`.

Also update `FontCache::GetGenericFamilyNameForScript()` to switch on
`GetScriptForFont()` and resolve `USCRIPT_ARABIC_NASTALIQ` generic
families using `U+06CC` so Nastaliq-preferring locales use the Nastaliq
typeface (and its vertical metrics) as the primary font.

The changes are under the `NastaliqScript` runtime flag.

Reland "[Indigo] Add \"NEW\" badge to \"Try it on\" context menu item"

This is a reland of commit f534941bbbf5ba7dd4786dfb1dceb0dee70ed2ca

Fix: In
RenderViewContextMenuPrefsTest.IndigoContextMenuEligibilityAndExecution,
null-check UserEducationService::new_badge_controller() before
initializing it and checking IsNewFeatureAt. On Chrome for Testing
builds (e.g. mac-rel-cft),
UserEducationServiceFactory::ProfileAllowsUserEducation() returns false
by default, leaving new_badge_controller() null.

Original change's description:
> [Indigo] Add "NEW" badge to "Try it on" context menu item
>
> Registers features::kIndigoContextMenu in MaybeRegisterChromeNewBadges
> and connects SetIsNewFeatureAt and MaybeNotifyNewBadgeFeatureUsed in
> RenderViewContextMenu.
>

[Media] Require consistent resource length and don't retry SW failures

When a UrlData already has a known length from an earlier response,
reject subsequent 200 OK or 206 Partial Content responses (or
UrlData::MergeFrom merges) that report a different or unspecified
length. This prevents a service worker from splicing responses from two
different same-origin resources of different sizes onto the same
UrlData, which previously allowed leaking the second resource's length
either via DidFinishLoading()'s short-body retry Range header or by
overwriting UrlData::length() on a mid-resource 200 OK.

Additionally, do not retry failed loads in DidFail() when the response
was fetched via a service worker.

Fixed: 573265164
